First run
  1. The panel answers on this server only, at http://127.0.0.1:8080/
     To reach it from your own computer, forward the port over SSH and open
     that same address there:
       ssh -L 8080:127.0.0.1:8080 root@THIS_SERVER
  2. Open Websites. The panel asks for the operator access key, then for a
     username, a password and an authenticator app. The key is in this file,
     which only root and sifpanel can read. Open it on the server; do not
     paste it into chat, tickets or screenshots:
       /var/lib/sifpanel/state/operator/access.key
  3. Websites need TCP 80 and 443 open in your firewall. The installer does
     not change firewall rules. To sign in to the panel from other machines
     without an SSH forward, and to use phpMyAdmin (it opens on the panel's
     HTTPS address, on port 8444), run this again with --panel-host NAME.

Rollback
  Before you close this SSH session, open a second one and check that you
  can still sign in.
  1. Sifruno's units, first: the broker does not run without the nsswitch
     change.
       systemctl disable --now sifruno.service sifruno-broker.service
       rm /etc/systemd/system/sifruno.service /etc/systemd/system/sifruno-broker.service
       systemctl daemon-reload
  2. sshd policy: /etc/ssh/sshd_config.d/40-sifruno.conf (created in this run)
       rm /etc/ssh/sshd_config.d/40-sifruno.conf
       sshd -t && systemctl reload sshd
  3. nsswitch: /etc/nsswitch.conf
       cp -a /etc/nsswitch.conf.bak-20261125-2013 /etc/nsswitch.conf
  4. OpenLiteSpeed: /usr/local/lsws/conf/httpd_config.conf
       cp -a /usr/local/lsws/conf/httpd_config.conf.bak-20261125-2013 /usr/local/lsws/conf/httpd_config.conf && systemctl restart lsws
  5. Process limit for hosting accounts in PAM sessions: /etc/security/limits.d/90-sifruno-accounts.conf
       rm /etc/security/limits.d/90-sifruno-accounts.conf
     The broker's per-account slice limits are in
     /etc/systemd/system/user-<uid>.slice.d/50-sifruno-limits.conf; with the broker
     stopped, remove those files and run systemctl daemon-reload.
  6. phpMyAdmin: take the line "include /usr/local/lsws/conf/sifruno-phpmyadmin.conf" out of /usr/local/lsws/conf/httpd_config.conf
     (step 4's backup is from before it), then
       rm -f /usr/local/lsws/conf/sifruno-phpmyadmin.conf && rm -rf /usr/local/lsws/conf/vhosts/sifruno_phpmyadmin /usr/local/lib/sifruno/phpmyadmin /etc/sifruno/phpmyadmin
       systemctl reload lsws
     The panel drops its temporary database users (SifrunoPMA_...) within
     the hour while it runs.
  Packages, the sifpanel and sifpma users, websites and databases
  stay. Remove them by hand if you want them gone.
